view lives in plone.app.layout and is registered\n+on the ``IPloneAppLayoutLayer`` browser layer (installed by the\n+``plone.app.layout:default`` profile).  Like the other write-enabled forms it\n+must be protected against CSRF attacks.  This test used to live in\n+``Products.CMFPlone/tests/csrf.txt`` and moved here together with the view.\n+\n+First we need a logged in user with manager rights:\n+\n+  >>> import transaction; transaction.commit()\n+  >>> from plone.app.testing import SITE_OWNER_NAME\n+  >>> from plone.app.testing import SITE_OWNER_PASSWORD\n+  >>> from plone.testing.zope import Browser\n+  >>> app = layer[\'app\']\n+  >>> portal = layer[\'portal\']\n+  >>> browser = Browser(app)\n+  >>> browser.handleErrors = False\n+  >>> browser.open(\'http://nohost/plone/login_form\')\n+  >>> browser.getControl(name=\'__ac_name\').value = SITE_OWNER_NAME\n+  >>> browser.getControl(name=\'__ac_password\').value = SITE_OWNER_PASSWORD\n+  >>> browser.getControl(\'Log in\').click()\n+\n+Now we add a folder to share:\n+\n+  >>> browser.open(\'http://nohost/plone/\')\n+  >>> browser.getLink(url=\'++add++Folder\').click()\n+  >>> browser.getControl(\'Title\').value = \'a folder\'\n+  >>> browser.getControl(\'Save\').click()\n+  >>> browser.url\n+  \'http://nohost/plone/a-folder/view\'\n+\n+Reopen URL to clean up HTTP_REFERRER\n+\n+  >>> browser.open(\'http://nohost/plone/a-folder/\')\n+\n+"Sharing" the item via the toolbar link:\n+\n+  >>> browser.getLink(\'Sharing\').click()\n+  >>> browser.url\n+  \'http://nohost/plone/a-folder/@@sharing?_auth...\'\n+  >>> browser.getControl(name=\'entries.role_Editor:records\').value\n+  []\n+\n+Change the value of the second _authenticator and check for Exception\n+\n+  >>> browser.getControl(name=\'_authenticator\', index=1).value = \'invalid!\'\n+  >>> browser.getControl(name=\'entries.role_Editor:records\').value = [\'True\']\n+  >>> browser.getControl(\'Save\').click()\n+  Traceback (most recent call last):\n+  ...\n+  zExceptions.Forbidden\n+\n+With a valid authenticator the change goes through:\n+\n+  >>> \'http://nohost/plone/a-folder/@@sharing\'\n+  >>> browser.contents\n+  \'...Info...Changes saved...\'\n+  >>> browser.getControl(name=\'entries.role_Editor:records\').value\n+  [\'True\']\ndiff --git      one.\n+\n+        Keeping your email the same (which happens when you change something\n+        else on the personalize form) or changing it back to your login name,\n+        is fine.\n+\n+        So: we only return True if it is *really* a different email.\n+        """\n+        member = self.member\n+        if email in (member.getId(), member.getUserName()):\n+            return False\n+        # By default, PAS transforms login names to lowercase, at least when\n+        # email-as-login is used.  So compare the transformed/normalized names.\n+        pas = getToolByName(self.context, "acl_users")\n+        email_normalized = pas.applyTransform(email)\n+        # The user name should already have been normalized, but let\'s make sure.\n+        login_normalized = pas.applyTransform(member.getUserName())\n+        return email_normalized != login_normalized\n+\n+    def makeQuery(self):\n+ action(self):\n+        return self.request.getURL() + self.makeQuery()\n+\n+    def validate_email(self, action, data):\n+        error_keys = [error.field.getName() for error in notifyWidgetActionExecutionError(action, "portrait", err_str)\n+\n+    @button.buttonAndHandler(_("Save"))\n+    def handleSave(self, action):\n+        CheckAuthenticator(self.request)\n+ Please choose another."\n+                )\n+                notifyWidgetActionExecutionError(action, username_field, err_str)\n+\n+        if username_field not in error_keys:\n+            # Check the uniqueness of the login name, not only when\n+            # use_email_as_login is true, but always.\n+            pas = getToolByName(self, "acl_users")\n+            results = pas.searchUsers(name=login_name, exact_match=True)\n+            if results:\n+                err_str = _(\n+                    "The login name you selected is already in use or is not valid. (string): a path or orl\n+\n+    Returns:\n+        tuple: tuple of (uid, fragment) a fragment is an anchor id e.g. #head1\n+    """\n+    uid = None\n+    fragment = None\n+\n+    if not url:\n+        return uid, fragment\n+\n+    # resolve uid\n+    paths = url.split("/")\n+    paths_lower = [_item.lower() for _item in paths]\n+\n+    if "resolveuid" in paths_lower:\n+        ri = paths_lower.index("resolveuid")\n+        if ri + 1 != len(paths):\n+            uid = paths[ri + 1]\n+            if uid == "":\n+                uid = None\n+\n+    if not uid:\n+        return uid, fragment\n+\n+    # resolve fragment\n+    parts = urlparse(uid)\n+\n+    uid = parts.path\n+\n+    fragment = f"#{parts.fragment}" if parts.fragment else None\n+\n+    return uid, fragment\n+\n+\n+class LinkRedirectView(BrowserView):\n+    index = ViewPageTemplateFile("templates/link.pt")\n+\n+    def _url_uses_scheme(self, schemes, url=None):\n+        url = url or
